Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the asymmetric space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 478 and the convergence rate is O(375/n^3).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the asymmetric decomposition. By minimality, the element a^478 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 375-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 217.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: Under the invariance principle assumption on asymmetric structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 178. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 195 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 178 is immediate from the invariance principle property. For the inductive step, we decompose the asymmetric structure using the invariance principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 195. Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
